## 1. [MAYBE] [REFERRAL] Salesforce | Senior Data Scientist - GTM
- Link: https://salesforce.wd12.myworkdayjobs.com/External_Career_Site/job/California---San-Francisco/Senior-Data-Scientist---GTM_JR361638-1
- Location: 5 Locations
- Posted: Nov 10 (board gives the date only)
- Scanner fit: ~56 | Min YOE: 4 | H-1B (certified LCAs, Oct 2024-Dec 2025): 141 in data/analytics/finance occupations
- Type: full-time
- Title scope: core | Has: a/b test, dashboard, forecast, machine learning, python, r, spark, sql, statistic, ai agent, classification, quantitative, research, segmentation | Lacks: airflow, hadoop, salesforce, scala  | Note: title says Senior/II: asks 4 years referral company: ask your referral asks 4 years (referral stretch)
- Eligibility: CHECK | Please see our Candidate Privacy Statement for more information about how we use your personal data and your rights, including with regard to use of AI tools and opt out options.

### Job description

To get the best candidate experience, please consider applying for a maximum of 3 roles within 12 months to ensure you are not duplicating efforts. Job Category Data Job Details About Salesforce Salesforce is the #1 AI CRM, where humans with agents drive customer success together. Here, ambition meets action. Tech meets trust. And innovation isn’t a buzzword — it’s a way of life. The world of work as we know it is changing and we're looking for Trailblazers who are passionate about bettering business and the world through AI, driving innovation, and keeping Salesforce's core values at the heart of it all. Ready to level-up your career at the company leading workforce transformation in the agentic era? You’re in the right place! Agentforce is the future of AI, and you are the future of Salesforce. About the Role We are looking for an experienced Senior Data Scientist to support Slack's Go-to-Market (GTM) organization. In this role, you will partner with Sales Strategy, Sales Programs, Product, Finance, and Data teams to uncover opportunities that accelerate growth, improve customer outcomes, and shape GTM strategy through data. You will work on some of Slack's highest-impact business questions, applying advanced analytics, experimentation, and statistical methods to understand customer behavior, evaluate strategic initiatives, and optimize GTM performance. As Slack continues to invest in AI-powered selling and customer intelligence, you will help define the analytical frameworks, predictive models, and measurement systems that enable smarter decisions at scale. You will collaborate closely with Engineers, Business Leaders, and Researchers to identify opportunities, influence strategic direction, and build a culture of evidence-based decision making. Slack has a positive, diverse, and supportive culture. We look for people who are curious, inventive, and strive to improve every day. In our work together, we aim to be smart, humble, hardworking, and, above all, collaborative. If this sounds like a great fit, we'd love to hear from you. What You Will Be Doing Partner with GTM leadership to identify strategic opportunities, evaluate business performance, and influence key business decisions. Apply statistical methods, experimentation, and advanced analytics to understand customer behavior, sales performance, and drivers of business growth. Develop predictive models, segmentation frameworks, and forecasting methodologies that improve GTM planning and execution. Design measurement strategies and success metrics to evaluate products, programs, and strategic initiatives. Conduct deep-dive analyses to identify opportunities for revenue growth, customer adoption, operational efficiency, and field productivity. Build scalable analytical frameworks that uncover trends, quantify business impact, and support executive decision making. Partner with Data Engineering to develop trusted datasets and scalable analytical foundations that enable advanced modeling and experimentation. Collaborate with Product and Sales Strategy to develop intelligence solutions that connect account health, product usage, customer engagement, and seller actions into actionable recommendations. Translate complex analyses into compelling narratives, executive presentations, dashboards, and written recommendations tailored to both technical and non-technical audiences. Champion evidence-based decision making by improving access to trusted metrics, analytical methodologies, and strategic insights across the GTM organization. What You Should Have 4+ years of industry experience applying data science, statistics, or quantitative analysis to solve complex business problems. Advanced proficiency in SQL and at least one programming language for data science, such as Python, R, or Scala. Knowledge of workflow orchestration tools like Apache Airflow is highly desirable. Strong foundation in statistics, experimentation, causal inference, predictive modeling, and analytical problem solving. Experience working with large-scale data technologies such as Spark, Presto, Hive, Hadoop, or similar distributed data platforms. Proven ability to communicate complex analytical findings clearly to executive and cross-functional audiences. Experience partnering with Product, Sales, Finance, or other GTM organizations to influence strategic decisions. A related technical degree required. Preferred Qualifications Master's degree or PhD in Statistics, Computer Science, Economics, Mathematics, Operations Research, or a related quantitative discipline. Experience supporting Sales, Marketing, Customer Success, Finance, or other Go-to-Market organizations through advanced analytics. Expertise in experimental design, A/B testing, causal inference, forecasting, or machine learning. Experience developing production-quality analytical models and partnering with engineering teams to operationalize data science solutions. Deep understanding of GTM operating models and experience collaborating with Sales Strategy and Sales Programs teams. Demonstrated ability to independently define ambiguous business problems, develop rigorous analytical approaches, and influence executive-level strategy. Experience applying AI and machine learning techniques to improve business decision making, operational efficiency, or customer experiences. Unleash Your Potential When you join Salesforce, you’ll be limitless in all areas of your life. Our benefits and resources support you to find balance and be your best , and our AI agents accelerate your impact so you can do your best . Together, we’ll bring the power of Agentforce to organizations of all sizes and deliver amazing experiences that customers love. Apply today to not only shape the future — but to redefine what’s possible — for yourself, for AI, and the world. Accommodations If you need a

### Job description

Job Category Operations Job Details About Salesforce Salesforce is the #1 AI CRM, where humans with agents drive customer success together. Here, ambition meets action. Tech meets trust. What You'll Be Doing & Why It Matters A blend of strategy and operational execution, the Strategy & Operations Sr Analyst partners with Renewals Leadership as a trusted advisor, focused on executing the hierarchy, as well as attrition forecasting motion. This role requires deep alignment with multiple levels of the Renewals cross-functional alignment with Sales and Customer Success organizations. This role supports the weekly forecasting process and is responsible for managing content related to the forecast. This person leverages insights to increase accuracy and efficiency in the retention forecast across different operating units. You are detail-oriented and feel fulfilled when helping leaders run their business! We're looking for highly passionate candidates who have relentless curiosity, a startup mentality, attention to detail, willingness to manage multiple priorities, and ability to deal with ambiguity effectively. This is an individual contributor role reporting to the Director, Renewals Strategy & Operations. Core Responsibilities & Scope Support the weekly OU forecast process across Renewals Drive forecast excellence by coordinating prep for weekly OU forecast calls Partner with the Analytics team and perform ad-hoc analyses across multiple data sets and tools (examples of analyses include customer segmentation, participation, propensity to buy, white space, likelihood to churn, etc.) to drive forecast accuracy or improve forecast recommendations Evaluate ongoing OU business performance against Targets Create executive-level presentations for both local and global leadership reviews Minimum Requirements 2+ years of professional experience, ideally in Sales strategy/business operations roles Experience with forecasting, business analysis, and data visualization Strong business acumen and stakeholder management Degree or equivalent relevant experience required. Experience will be evaluated based on the core competencies for the role (e.g. extracurricular leadership roles, military experience, volunteer roles, work experience, etc.) Excellent presentation and executive engagement skills Outstanding written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to craft compelling stories Self-starter and high degree of motivation to go above and beyond the task at hand and succeed in a collaborative, fast-paced environment. Ability to translate complex information into easily consumable insights Working knowledge of Salesforce Intermediate or better mastery of Snowflake/SQL and Excel/G-sheets Beginner or better mastery of Tableau Flexible schedule, the ability to work with our global Renewals teams in different time zones. We Do Consulting Differently Apply your doctoral research skills to economic questions arising in litigation and business decisions. BRG’s Economics, Disputes & Investigations practice invites fourth- and fifth-year economics PhD students to join its summer cohort. As a summer intern, you will work alongside economists and experts to apply economic theory and empirical methods to client matters. BRG brings together academic researchers and practitioners to address complex economic disputes. Engagements include antitrust and competition, intellectual property, and securities and financial markets. You will contribute to project teams and see how economic research informs expert analysis in litigation and consulting. The summer cohort offers an opportunity to explore economic consulting before the academic job market. You will bring your research perspective to applied problems and develop experience explaining analytical findings to audiences beyond academia. Reviewing academic literature and industry evidence, documenting methods, and checking analyses for accuracy and reproducibility. Experience applying econometric methods to research questions, with an understanding of identification and the limitations of empirical evidence. Interest in applying economic research to litigation and consulting matters. Relevant internship or work experience is welcome but not required. Ability to work independently on a defined research task and collaborate effectively within a project team. Experience using artificial intelligence tools to support analytical workflows, with careful validation of outputs and attention to confidentiality. Evidence of research ability through dissertation work, working papers, or other research projects; published articles are not required. Proficiency in statistical programming, such as Stata, R, or Python, and experience working with research datasets. Familiarity with SQL or Microsoft Excel is useful. Strong writing and analytical reasoning, with the ability to explain methods and findings clearly to technical and nontechnical audiences. To qualify, candidates must expect to complete their PhD by spring 2028. Please submit a résumé, transcripts, one writing sample and a letter of recommendation through the link below by December 31, 2026 Salary Range: $40-$60 per hour. Our culture centers on agility and connectivity which sets us apart and gets you ahead. ### Job description

Argonne National Laboratory is seeking a Predoctoral Appointee to support tool development and data management for the Techno-Economic Analysis Group within the Energy Systems and Infrastructure Analysis (ESIA) Division. The successful candidate will contribute to the development, maintenance, and modernization of analytical tools used to evaluate the costs and performance of industrial, power, and transportation technologies and infrastructure, primarily ESTEEM. The successful candidate will focus on supporting the development of the Energy Systems Techno-Economic Evaluation Model (ESTEEM). The candidate will expand ESTEEM to enable the study of potential technical, economic, and environmental benefits of the production, transport, and delivery of various commodities across the industrial, transportation, and power sectors. Working with Argonne researchers and technical collaborators, the predoctoral appointee will support the conversion of existing Excel-based techno-economic models into Python modules for a centralized modeling platform. Responsibilities will include reviewing and documenting existing model calculations, assumptions, and data dependencies; translating spreadsheet calculations into modular, reusable Python code; and testing Python implementations against existing models to verify consistency and accuracy. The appointee will assist with codebase architecture and organization, including shared calculation modules, standardized model inputs and outputs, configuration management, and interfaces between models and databases. The predoctoral appointee will also support the development and maintenance of technical datasets and databases, including data cleaning, validation, integration, version tracking, and documentation of data sources and assumptions. Additional responsibilities will include developing automated tests and quality-control checks; troubleshooting model and data issues; supporting updates to model parameters and datasets; and preparing technical documentation, user guidance, presentations, and progress summaries. The successful candidate will collaborate with researchers to translate analytical requirements into maintainable software and support the consistent implementation of techno-economic calculations across the modeling platform. Position Requirements Recent or soon-to-be completed Master’s degree in engineering, computer science, data science, applied mathematics, economics, or a related field. Proficiency in Python, including experience developing modular code and using libraries for data processing and numerical analysis, such as pandas and NumPy. Experience working with Excel-based models, including interpreting formulas, tracing calculation dependencies, and managing structured input and output data. Knowledge of software development practices, including code organization, reusable components, version control, testing, debugging, and documentation. Knowledge of database concepts and experience organizing, querying, validating, and maintaining technical datasets. Analytical and quantitative skills to interpret model calculations, reconcile results across implementations, and identify inconsistencies in data or assumptions. Ability to translate technical requirements and existing spreadsheet workflows into clear, maintainable Python implementations. Oral and written communication skills to explain technical issues, document model functionality, and communicate progress to researchers and other collaborators. Time-management and organizational skills to support multiple projects, meet deadlines, and track assigned deliverables. Ability to work independently on assigned tasks and collaboratively in a multidisciplinary team environment. Ability to model Argonne’s core values of impact, safety, respect, integrity, and teamwork. This position requires an on-site presence at the Argonne campus in Lemont, Illinois. Preferred qualifications: Experience converting Excel-based engineering or economic models to Python. Familiarity with SQL, relational databases, and data integration workflows. Experience with Git-based collaborative development, automated testing, and continuous integration. Familiarity with techno-economic analysis concepts, such as capital and operating costs, cash-flow analysis, levelized costs, and sensitivity analysis. Experience supporting shared modeling platforms, including common data structures, model interfaces, and configuration-driven workflows. Job Family Temporary Job Profile Predoctoral Appointee Worker Type Long-Term (Fixed Term) Time Type Full time The expected hiring range for this position is $58,297.00-$97,161.00. Please note that the pay range information is a general guideline only. The pay offered to a selected candidate will be determined based on factors such as, but not limited to, the scope and responsibilities of the position, the qualifications of the selected candidate, business considerations, internal equity, and external market pay for comparable jobs. Additionally, comprehensive benefits are part of the total rewards package. Click here to view Argonne employee benefits! As an equal employment opportunity employer, and in accordance with our core values of impact, safety, respect, integrity and teamwork, Argonne National Laboratory is committed to a safe and welcoming workplace that fosters collaborative scientific discovery and innovation. Argonne encourages everyone to apply for employment. Argonne is committed to nondiscrimination and considers all qualified applicants for employment without regard to any characteristic protected by law. Argonne employees, and certain guest researchers and contractors, are subject to particular restrictions related to participation in Foreign Government Sponsored or Affiliated Activities, as defined and detailed in United States Department of Energy Order 486.1A. You will be asked to disclose any such participation in the application phase for review by Argonne's Legal Department. All Argonne offers of employment are contingent upon a background check that includes an assessment of criminal conviction history conducted on an individualized and case-by-case basis. Please be advised that Argonne positions require upon hire (or may require in the future) for the individual be to obtain a government access authorization that involves additional background check requirements. Failure to obtain or maintain such government access authorization could result in the withdrawal of a job offer or future termination of employment.

The problems are complex, adversarial, and constantly evolving. Models must identify emerging fraud patterns, scams, account takeovers, payment abuse, and other forms of financial risk while minimizing disruption to legitimate customers. Success is not measured only through offline model metrics. It is measured through prevented losses, improved approval rates, reduced false positives, faster investigations, and more reliable customer experience. This role sits within a multidisciplinary risk team of machine learning engineers, data scientists, risk strategy specialists, analytics engineers, product managers, and operations teams. Improve data quality, features, and model performance, balancing fraud detection with customer experience. Develop AI agents to support investigations, summarize cases, retrieve evidence, and automate routine workflows. Use AI coding tools to accelerate development, testing, and debugging while reviewing and validating their output. Work with engineering, product, and risk teams to deploy solutions and monitor their impact. &nbsp; What We Look For In You A strong foundation in machine learning, with hands-on experience in deep learning and tree-based models such as XGBoost or LightGBM. Strong Python skills and experience training, evaluating, and deploying models. Some practical experience building AI agents or LLM applications, including tool calling, retrieval, or workflow automation. Prototypes and personal projects are welcome. Regular use of AI coding tools, with the ability to understand, test, and improve generated code. Familiarity with databases and data pipelines for preparing training data and supporting ML workflows. Good problem-solving, communication, and collaboration skills. &nbsp; Nice to Haves Experience in account takeover (ATO), scam detection, or other fraud and risk use cases. Experience with LLM training, fine-tuning, evaluation, and alignment. Experience with reinforcement learning algorithms and their practical implementation. Experience with real-time ML systems, imbalanced datasets, or model explainability. Prior fraud or risk experience is helpful Fluency in Mandarin &nbsp; Perks &amp; Benefits Competitive total compensation package L&amp;D programs for employees' growth and development Various team building programs and company events Wellness and meal allowances Comprehensive healthcare schemes for employees and dependants More that we love to tell you along the process! &nbsp; OKX Statement: OKX is committed to equal employment opportunities regardless of race, color, genetic information, creed, religion, sex, sexual orientation, gender identity, lawful alien status, national origin, age, marital status, and non-job related physical or mental disability, or protected veteran status. 7-Eleven is an iconic family of brands with over 86,000 locations, surpassing every retailer in the world. We revolutionize convenience, restaurants and fuel through cutting edge innovation — working hard to be the customer's first choice. 7-Eleven empowers our employees to "activate awesome" and make a meaningful impact in their stores and communities every day. If you're ready to grow, lead and make a difference, come join our team and help shape the future of convenience. JOB SUMMARY The Merchandising Support Analyst provides analytical, process, and execution support to the Merchandising organization by turning cost, vendor, category, and performance data into clear insights and actionable recommendations. This role partners closely with Merchandising teams, Strategic Sourcing, and cross-functional business partners to support supplier strategy, cost management, vendor performance, and category decision-making. The analyst will help build fact-based materials, dashboards, and business cases that support cost mitigation, vendor negotiations, and category development decisions. KEY DUTIES AND RESPONSIBILITIES • Support the Cost Policy process by modeling the financial impact of vendor cost changes, analyzing cost movement, and providing insights that support cost mitigation and negotiation decisions. • Manage and track cross-functional stages of cost policy, vendor submission reviews, and related process requirements to ensure adherence to guidelines and timelines. • Regularly engage with Merchandising teams and cross-functional business partners to provide initiative-taking insights, respond to analytical requests, and support merchandise initiatives. • Prepare cost, vendor, and category materials for leadership reviews, management decks, and recurring business updates. • Develop, synthesize, and maintain Merchandise and vendor scorecards by tracking performance, interpreting results, and identifying actionable category development opportunities. • Analyze multiple data points, including cost changes, commodity movement, vendor performance, and category trends, to create a clear business storyline and recommendation. • Support development and evolution of multi-year vendor and merchandise strategies aligned to strategic goals, including vendor evaluation, assortment considerations, and category opportunities. • Build dashboards, trackers, and reporting tools that help stakeholders understand vendor cost movement, cost mitigation progress, and key business drivers. • Provide excellent customer service to the Merchandising organization through accurate analytics, timely execution, and strong follow-through. Support Joint Business Planning (JBP) activities through supplier analytics, performance tracking, fact-based negotiation materials, and identification of cost recovery or growth opportunities. KEY LEADERSHIP COMPETENCIES • Communicates findings and recommendations clearly and concisely to Merchandising, category managers, sourcing managers, and cross-functional stakeholders. • Uses data to assess business situations, identify root causes, and recommend practical solutions with appropriate business context. • Builds trusted working relationships with Merchandising teams and business partners by being responsive, accurate, and solutions oriented. • Adapts quickly to shifting priorities, new business needs, and evolving merchandise processes while maintaining strong execution quality. • Works independently while also collaborating effectively in a team environment. EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE EDUCATION: Bachelor's degree (or equivalent experience) in Business, Business Analytics, Economics, Finance, Supply Chain, or related field. PREFERRED EDUCATION: Master's degree preferred but not required. YEARS OF RELEVANT WORK EXPERIENCE: 2+ years of experience utilizing analytics, financial modeling, category/vendor analysis, or business reporting to support decisions. CERTIFICATIONS / LICENSES: NA SPECIFIC KNOWLEDGE AND SKILLS • Ability to thrive in a demanding environment, manage multiple competing priorities, and execute with an elevated level of accuracy. • Strong analytical skills with the ability to combine multiple data sources into a clear business case and practical recommendation. • Experience building and analyzing financial models, cost scenarios, dashboards, scorecards, and management reporting materials. • Proficiency with Microsoft Office Suite, especially Excel and PowerPoint; experience with Power BI, Circana, Datalake, SQL/query tools, Oracle reporting, or other data tools preferred. • Strong written and verbal communication skills, including the ability to translate data-driven insights for non-technical audiences. • Excellent critical thinking skills and attention to detail, with a focus on analytics, execution, and verification. • Customer-service mindset and ability to support Merchandising stakeholders with timely, accurate, and actionable analysis. • Function as an early warning system by proactively identifying supplier, cost, promotional, and risks before they become business issues. Translate complex data into executive-ready insights that help leaders quickly understand exposure, opportunities, recommended actions, and expected business impact. Provide visibility to decision ownership, action plans, and progress tracking to improve accountability and accelerate issue resolution across cross-functional teams. #LI-CV1 If an hourly or salary range is included in this ad it represents the range 7-Eleven in good faith believes is the range of compensation for this role at the time of this posting. LiteLLM is the world's most popular AI Gateway, trusted by companies like Adobe, Netflix, and NASA. We're building the next generation of infrastructure for AI applications—and now we're building our second product.





WHAT YOU WILL BE WORKING ON

Skills: Python, LLM APIs, FastAPI

As the ML Engineer, you'll be responsible for ensuring LiteLLM unifies the format for calling LLM APIs in the broader OpenAI + Anthropic spec. This involves writing transformations to convert API requests from OpenAI/Anthropic spec to various LLM provider formats, building provider-agnostic unification functionality (e.g. session management across non-openai models for /v1/responses API, etc.). You'll work directly with the CEO and CTO on critical projects including:

 - System design for supporting provider level features at the gateway level (skills, compaction, batches, etc.)

 - Thinking about the developer experience - to support millions of users who leverage your work via LiteLLM's Python SDK

 - Building across LLM's, MCP's, and Agents by maintaining an excellent LLM interoperability layer, supporting a wide range of MCP Auth flows and enabling various Agent use-cases

What is our tech stack

The tech stack includes Python, FastAPI, Redis, Postgres.





WHO WE ARE LOOKING FOR

 - 1-2 years of backend/full-stack experience with production systems

 - Passion for open source and user engagement

 - Experience working with the OpenAI api (understand the difference between /chat/completions and /responses, and can speak to API-specific nuances)

 - Strong work ethic and ability to thrive in small teams

 - Eagerness to talk to users and help solve real problems





WHY JOIN LITELLM?

 - Work directly with the founders every day.

 - Build products used by thousands of engineering teams around the world.

 - Own meaningful product decisions—not just implementation.

 - Move incredibly fast and see your work in production immediately.

 - Help define the future of AI infrastructure and developer tooling.

 - Competitive salary, equity, health, dental, and vision benefits.



ABOUT LITELLM

LiteLLM is the world's most widely adopted AI Gateway. We provide a unified API for 100+ LLM providers along with routing, authentication, budgets, observability, and governance for production AI systems. Thousands of companies rely on LiteLLM to power AI in production.

If you love talking to users, building products, and shipping software that people actually use, we'd love to meet you.

Anduril Industries is a defense technology company with a mission to transform U.S. and allied military capabilities with advanced technology. By bringing the expertise, technology, and business model of the 21st century’s most innovative companies to the defense industry, Anduril is changing how military systems are designed, built and sold. Anduril’s family of systems is powered by Lattice OS, an AI-powered operating system that turns thousands of data streams into a realtime, 3D command and control center. As the world enters an era of strategic competition, Anduril is committed to bringing cutting-edge autonomy, AI, computer vision, sensor fusion, and networking technology to the military in months, not years. ABOUT THE JOB The Production Master Data Engineer will design and build the tools that keep master data accurate across Anduril's production operations. Rather than fixing data issues one at a time, this role builds the automations, integrations, and validation controls that prevent them. The Engineer will partner with Production teams, IT, and Business Systems to scope and deliver scalable master data solutions across multiple business systems. This position is hands-on and execution-focused, shipping practical solutions quickly and improving them as Anduril's production operations scale. WHAT YOU’LL DO Design and own automations, bots, and data pipelines (Python, APIs, scheduled jobs) that replace manual master data processes, including error handling, version control, and ongoing enhancements. Implement automated validation rules and monitoring that catch master data errors before they impact production. Integrate PLM, ERP, MES, and other production systems to keep master data, engineering changes, and revisions synchronized. Investigate systemic data issues and engineer permanent fixes. Apply AI/LLM-based solutions to streamline data enrichment, classification, and self-service master data requests. Deliver dashboards and metrics that give stakeholders visibility into master data quality and its impact on production. Partner with Production teams to scope requirements and develop tools that close operational gaps. Lead data requirements, loads, and validation for system enhancements, upgrades, migrations, and new deployments. BASIC QUALIFICATIONS Bachelor's degree in Engineering, Computer Science, Information Systems, or a related field. 3+ years of experience in data engineering, master data management, or business systems supporting manufacturing or supply chain. Proficiency in Python and SQL for building and maintaining production-grade automations and data pipelines, using software engineering best practices (e.g., version control). Experience building REST API integrations between enterprise systems. Working knowledge of ERP master data and how it drives downstream production processes, with a strong commitment to data accuracy. PREFERRED QUALIFICATIONS Experience with data visualization and reporting tools such as Tableau, Power BI, or similar. Experience with large-scale ERP implementations. Experience with RPA or workflow automation platforms. Experience applying AI/LLM tools to data quality or automation. Experience in hardware development, aerospace and defense, or early-stage environments. ### Job description

The Central Science Team within Amazon’s People Experience and Technology org (PXTCS) uses economics, behavioral science, statistics, and machine learning to proactively identify mechanisms and process improvements which simultaneously improve Amazon and the lives, well-being, and the value of work to Amazonians. We are an interdisciplinary team, which combines the talents of science and engineering to develop and deliver solutions that measurably achieve this goal. We are looking for an Applied Scientist to build production machine learning solutions that improve employee experiences and inform workforce decisions. You will work on problems spanning predictive modeling, natural language processing, recommendation systems, personalization, generative AI, and causal evaluation. These solutions will predict workforce outcomes, identify individual and organizational needs, recommend targeted actions, and measure the causal impact of programs, products, and policies across diverse employee populations and business contexts. As an Applied Scientist, you will develop models and algorithms from prototype through production. You will collaborate with other scientists and economists to combine machine learning with causal inference and experimentation, allowing teams to understand both what is likely to happen and which actions will improve outcomes. You will also work with product managers, engineers, and business partners to translate business needs into technical solutions and communicate methods and results to technical and nontechnical audiences. Key job responsibilities • Design, develop, and deploy machine learning solutions in production environments. • Develop solutions using techniques that include predictive modeling, deep learning, natural language processing, recommendation systems, personalization, and generative AI. • Build models that predict workforce outcomes, identify employee and organizational needs, and recommend targeted actions. • Partner with economists and other scientists to design causal evaluations and experimental frameworks that measure the impact of programs, products, and policies. • Combine predictive models with causal estimates to help business partners identify effective actions and make evidence-based decisions. • Collaborate with product managers, engineers, and business partners to translate business requirements into modeling approaches and contribute to technical design and roadmap decisions. • Evaluate model accuracy, performance, calibration, and limitations, and identify opportunities for improvement. • Communicate technical concepts, methods, results, and recommendations to technical peers and business partners. A day in the life • Analyze data to investigate model performance or understand an unexpected result from a causal evaluation. • Brainstorm modeling and evaluation approaches with applied scientists and economists on the team. • Develop and test model features, recommendation methods, or generative AI applications. • Design or analyze experiments and observational studies to estimate the causal impact of programs, products, or policies. • Run simulations, offline evaluations, or backtests to assess model performance. • Meet with product, engineering, and business partners to review requirements, data, designs, and implementation decisions. • Review code changes or technical design documents from scientists and engineers. • Write and present documents covering methods, results, limitations, and recommendations. About the team The People eXperience and Technology Central Science Team (PXTCS) uses economics, behavioral science, statistics, and machine learning to proactively identify mechanisms and process improvements which simultaneously improve Amazon and the lives, wellbeing, and the value of work to Amazonians. We are an interdisciplinary team that combines the talents of science and engineering to develop and deliver solutions that measurably achieve this goal. - 3+ years of building models for business application experience - Experience programming in Java, C++, Python or related language - PhD, or Master's degree and 4+ years of applied research experience - Experience in professional software development - Experience in building machine learning models for business application - Experience in designing experiments and statistical analysis of results Amazon

Is accountable to the Principal Investigator(s) of the assigned research projects. Provides a variety of reports summarizing research data. Brown University Health employees are expected to successfully role model the organization's values of Compassion, Accountability, Respect, and Excellence as these values guide our everyday actions with patients, customers and one another. In addition to our values, all employees are expected to demonstrate the core Success Factors which tell us how we work together and how we get things done. Based on user feedback and program development, provide enhancements to assure optimal operation of data system, quality of data and user productivity. Develop and maintain reproducible SPSS scripts for data cleaning, processing, and analysis. Correct errors in scripts and data processes. Prepare data set for descriptive statistical analysis. Perform preliminary analysis, which would include frequencies, means and sums. Run established scoring of standard measures. In collaboration with statistician and/or PI, contribute to calculation of non-standardized measures. Provide ongoing status reports. At key time points provide preliminary analysis. Once data collection and data cleaning have been completed, provide access to finalized data set containing all variables and scoring. At a pre-determined time point, provide the PI with archived database and dataset to free up network space. Maintain up-to-date technical knowledge through seminars, academic courses and professional literature. Performs duties as assigned. MINIMUM QUALIFICATIONS: Minimum of Bachelor's Degree in Biostatistics, Statistics, Mathematics, Quantitative Methodology, Computer Science/MIS, Psychology, Public Health, or closely related field. EXPERIENCE: Equivalent to three years of progressively responsible information systems management background, including successful experience in programming, system analysis, data storage, documentation and management, preferably in a research or academic environment. Experience with human subjects research is emphasized, including preparing for and complying with HIPAA and IRB requirements. Experience interviewing participants and maintaining study records is preferred. Experience with REDCap or similar data capture systems is a plus. SUPERVISORY RESPONSIBILITY: None Pay Range: $48,068.80-$79,372.80

Job Summary The Commodities Quantitative Developer is responsible for designing, developing and supporting quantitative analytics for ConocoPhillips’ physical and financial commodity trading activities. Advanced level of proficiency integrating quantitative models with trading, risk, market data or reporting systems through APIs, services or data pipelines. Advanced level of proficiency reconciling analytical results across different models, systems and data sources. Advanced level of knowledge in analytical, problem-solving skills. Advanced level of knowledge in explaining quantitative concepts to both technical and commercial stakeholders. Strong ownership mindset, with a focus on reliability, controls and maintainability. - Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

About Monster Energy: Forget about blending in. That's not our style. We're the risk takers, the trailblazers, the gamechangers. We're not perfect and we don't pretend to be. We're raw, unfiltered, and a bit unconventional. Our drive is just like our athletes, unrivaled. The power is in your hands to define what success looks like and where you want to take your career. It's not just about what we do, but about who we become along the way. We are much more than a brand here. We are a way of life, a mindset. Join us. A Day in the Life: In the position of AI Data Engineer, you will engineer the data that Monster's artificial intelligence (AI) agents and copilots depend on, owning AI data readiness from source to answer. Assess and remediate data for specific AI use cases; build the retrieval pipelines, embeddings, and search indexes that ground Microsoft Copilot, Copilot Studio, Agentforce, and custom OpenAI and Claude-based agents; and maintain the semantic layer that links related records across systems (for example, a Salesforce account to its SAP financials and Power BI reporting). Working in Microsoft Fabric, Azure, and Databricks with the AI Architect, Sr. AI Solution Engineer, and AI Engineer, make enterprise data AI-ready, measurable, and trustworthy, so every AI answer can be traced to its source. The Impact You'll Make: Retrieval (RAG) Data Pipelines — Build and operate ingestion, parsing, chunking, enrichment, embedding, and vector / hybrid indexing pipelines (e.g., Azure AI Search, Databricks Vector Search) that ground Copilot, Copilot Studio, Agentforce, and custom agents; enforce permission-aware retrieval that respects source-system security. Semantic Layer &amp; Grounding Context — Design and maintain Power BI / Fabric semantic models, business glossary, and entity relationships (e.g., customer, product, and distributor across Salesforce, SAP, and BI) that give AI agents and Fabric data agents a shared, accurate understanding of the business. AI Data Readiness Assessment &amp; Remediation — Assess source data for each AI use case (coverage, quality, freshness, sensitivity, and bias); score AI readiness; and drive remediation with source owners before AI build begins. AI Data Access via MCP &amp; APIs — Expose curated, zero-copy data in Microsoft Fabric / OneLake and Databricks to AI agents through governed interfaces (e.g., MCP servers, SQL endpoints, APIs) with least-privilege access. Retrieval Quality, Observability &amp; Feedback — Monitor index freshness, data drift, and retrieval quality (relevance, groundedness, citation accuracy); feed agent logs and user feedback back into data fixes; publish AI-readiness key performance indicators (KPIs) in Power BI. Metadata, Lineage &amp; AI Data Governance — Register AI data assets in Microsoft Purview or Collibra; apply sensitivity labels; maintain lineage from source to index, agent, and answer; partner with Legal, Privacy, and Security on personal data, retention, EMEA data residency, and AI regulatory requirements. Evaluation &amp; Ground-Truth Datasets — Curate golden question-and-answer sets, labeled ground truth, and test data that measure AI accuracy; prepare data for fine-tuning or model customization when needed, including LLM-generated synthetic data with appropriate controls. LLM-Assisted Data Engineering &amp; Standards — Use OpenAI and Claude models and AI coding tools (e.g., Cursor) to automate metadata enrichment, entity matching, documentation, and pipeline code; contribute AI data standards, templates, and playbooks. When you join Verizon You want more out of a career. A place to share your ideas freely — even if they’re daring or different. Where the true you can learn, grow, and thrive. Our V Team is a community of people who anticipate, lead, and believe that listening is where learning begins. In crisis and in celebration, we come together — lifting our communities and building trust in how we show up, everywhere & always. Want in? Join the #VTeamLife. What you'll be doing… As a Distinguished Forward Deployed AI Engineer within Verizon’s Transformation Command Center (TCC), you will embed directly into the frontline of Verizon’s core Business Units to solve our most critical, ambiguous, and high-stakes operational challenges. Operating with total technical ownership, you will architect, build, and deploy production-grade agentic systems, enterprise RAG engines, full-stack microservices, and decision systems into live operations. You are a technical anchor that leads in industry: you pair deep systems craftsmanship with a product mindset, cutting through operational ambiguity to design production architectures, implement custom orchestration layers (via LangGraph, Temporal, or custom state machines), and enforce context management strategies that scale into enterprise assets. Beyond shipping point solutions, you will run workloads reliably on Domino, Airflow, and Kubernetes, abstracting bespoke code into reusable SDKs, shared libraries, and architectural playbooks that scale company-wide. Responsibilities include: End-to-End Architectural Blueprinting: Lead the design and definitions of the full-stack architecture for embedded AI and agentic initiatives, including multi-agent orchestration, state machines, latency/cost budgets, security boundaries, and enterprise back-end integrations. Agentic Orchestration Engineering, Workflow Automation and Governance: Architect stateful agent workflows complying with established agentic governance rules, using tools like LangGraph, Temporal, or event-driven state machines, establishing strict protocols for tool calling, execution sandboxing, and deterministic fallback logic. Context & Retrieval Systems: Lead the design of production RAG infrastructure incorporating hybrid search, re-ranking algorithms, metadata filtering, and semantic caching, directly informing and scaling Verizon’s context architecture. Runtime & Cloud Deployment: Deploy production agent services and scheduled workflows to target runtimes (e.g., Domino Data Lab, Apache Airflow, Kubernetes microservices, and serverless compute across AWS, GCP, or Azure). Evaluation Harness Design: Architect offline and online evaluation frameworks (e.g., LLM-as-a-judge, synthetic test generation, human-in-the-loop review queues, live A/B rollouts) to measure model drift, context hallucination rates, and task completion metrics. Platform Codification & IP Creation: Generalize custom domain deployments into reusable internal SDKs, API primitives, and modular UI components; mentor engineers across the organization and draft technical documentation for Verizon patent disclosures. Strategic Discovery & Scoping: Embed alongside BU leadership to map intricate operational processes, cut through organizational noise, and isolate the highest-leverage intervention points where applied AI delivers measurable uplift. Executive Translation: Serve as the technical authority in the room with BU Vice Presidents and Managing Directors, demystifying algorithmic behaviors, failure modes, and performance trade-offs. What we're looking for… You'll need to have: Bachelor’s degree in Computer Science, Software Engineering, or a related technical field or four or more years of work experience. Eight or more years shipping production software and/or demonstrated experience deploying LLM applications, agentic workflows, or autonomous tool-calling pipelines into production. Proven track record implementing both offline evaluation benchmarks and online evaluation/observability pipelines (latency, token costs, hallucination tracking, eval-driven development). Deep expertise in retrieval and context architectures: designing production RAG pipelines, vector databases (e.g., pgvector, Milvus, Pinecone), hybrid search (dense + sparse/lexical), and dynamic context management. Proven experience deploying containerized services and data workflows across enterprise platforms such as Domino Data Lab, Apache Airflow, Kubernetes (EKS/GKE), and hyperscaler cloud environments (AWS/GCP/Azure). Advanced full-stack programming experience and mastery in Python, SQL, and modern JavaScript/TypeScript stacks. Proven track record designing offline/online eval harnesses, guardrail frameworks, and observability systems (latency, token optimization, tracing). Demonstrated ability to independently scope ambiguous technical initiatives and present architectural designs to senior executive stakeholders. Demonstrated ability to interface directly with enterprise stakeholders, translate ambiguous business requirements into technical architectures, and lead project delivery end-to-end. Even better if you have one or more of the following: Master’s degree in Computer Science, Machine Learning, Robotics, or a related technical field. Experience building and scaling centralized context platforms or enterprise search engines similar to Verizon’s internal enterprise context layer platforms. Prior forward-deployed engineering, solutions architecture, or technical consulting experience in top-tier technology firms or hyperscalers. Record of turning one-off operational builds into enterprise-wide shared libraries, with associated technical publications or filed patents. Domain knowledge in telecommunications, customer growth and retention, customer segmentation or large-scale customer care operations. If Verizon and this role sound like a fit for you, we encourage you to apply even if you don’t meet every “even better” qualification listed above. Analysts act as the primary support to Bridgewater’s Strategists, a small group of senior investors appointed by Bridgewater’s co-CIOs to partner directly with our clients on their most important Research priorities. Day-to-day work includes collaborating with Strategists, Client Advisors, asset class teams, and our CIOs to define approaches on how to handle different client-focused research projects and execute on them through research, analysis, and project management, culminating in novel investment insights that informs out clients’ thinking. From their unique position at the heart of Bridgewater’s client-focused research activity, these individuals have an aerial view into our ever-evolving perspectives on world markets, high-impact company-wide projects and our investment philosophy and processes.&nbsp; &nbsp; Bridgewater’s market-driven approach to client service requires our Analysts to continually expand their understanding and knowledge of the markets. Through formal training and informal on-the-job instruction, Analysts become well-versed in our investment approach, process and different strategies, our book of business, and our production systems.&nbsp; &nbsp; As a Client Investment Research Analyst for the Investor Relations team, you will partner with Client Advisors and Strategists to provide clients with insights and advice to address their questions about Bridgewater’s strategies, performance, and portfolio positions. Candidates must be driven, confident, and goal-oriented. All Bridgewater employees are expected to be honest, exceptionally direct, and eager to provide and receive objective feedback. Our employees constantly strive for self-improvement through feedback and self-reflection and are committed to the pursuit of excellence. About Bridgewater &nbsp; Bridgewater Associates is a global macro investment firm, founded in 1975, whose mission is to develop a fundamental, cause-and-effect understanding of how markets and economies work—and translate that understanding into insights, returns, and solutions that help the world’s leading institutional investors solve their most important investment challenges. &nbsp; Over five decades, we have codified our fundamental understanding into a systematic investment process—integrating human judgment with machine intelligence with the goal of applying our thinking consistently, testing it against reality, and continuously improving decision-making over time. ### Job description

Applied ML Engineer

The Opportunity

You'll own the last mile — taking promising AI/ML concepts from research and making them reliable, scalable, and production-functional. As an Applied ML Engineer at Nascent, you'll sit at the intersection of a strong R&D team and the systems that consume their work, bringing structured engineering discipline to close the gap between prototype and production. The split is roughly 75% deployment and applied engineering, 25% participating in R&D. Your job is to make things work, stay working, and scale.

The team is full of strong researchers and ML minds. We are targeting experience in a professional engineering environment where architecture decisions matter, task management is a given, and shared ownership of systems is how you build. You'll bring that discipline and push the team's engineering standards forward. You'll be the person who says "here's how we'd build this properly" and then builds it.

You'll thrive here if you're a builder who gets energy from taking messy, promising work and turning it into something maintainable and production-grade — and if you want to do that in a high-autonomy, performance-driven environment where your output shows up in P&L. This role is remote-first; overlap with U.S. business hours is expected.

Responsibilities

 - Take research-quality and prototype ML code from the R&D team and refactor it into production-ready, maintainable, deployable systems — closing the gap between "it works" and "it runs reliably at scale."

 - Own end-to-end deployment of ML pipelines: data ingestion, feature engineering, model serving, monitoring, and reliability.

 - Integrate structured development practices — task management (Jira/Linear), Git workflows, code reviews, CI/CD — on a team that moves fast and needs the discipline to match.

 - Collaborate daily with researchers and the rest of the team to translate their intent into working, observable, production systems.

 - Identify and call out architectural and engineering quality issues; be the voice that says "this is how we'd do it properly" and then does it.

 - Build and maintain monitoring, logging, and alerting infrastructure so the team knows when things break before the trading desk does.

 - Support the team's transition toward Rust-native infrastructure as the codebase evolves.



About you

 - You came up in a real code shop — a structured, team-based engineering environment where architecture decisions were debated, code reviews were rigorous, and task management was just how work got done. Not focused on solo projects. Not academia. A professional dev environment.

 - You are a builder first. Research is interesting to you, but shipping is what gets you up in the morning. You take messy, promising work and make it production-grade — and you take pride in that.

 - You are collaborative by nature. You've worked alongside strong engineers, shared ownership of systems, and given and received hard feedback on code. You know what good looks like because you've seen it.

 - You have strong opinions about how software should be built, and you're not shy about expressing them — but you back it up with work, not just words.

 - You are curious about AI and ML systems — not necessarily an expert, but genuinely interested in how they work and eager to learn from a team that lives in this space.

 - You don't need all the answers handed to you. High autonomy, fast-moving environment, ambiguous problems — that's where you do your best work.

 - Finance doesn't need to be your background, but you're curious about markets.



Preferred experience

 - Rust proficiency or strong willingness to ramp quickly — the team is moving Rust-native and needs someone who can read and eventually contribute to Rust codebases.

 - Exposure to ML/AI concepts — model training, inference pipelines, or working alongside ML researchers — even if not as a primary specialization.

 - Experience with NLP or text-based signal extraction as an applied engineering discipline.

 - Experience in or curiosity about financial markets

 - Familiarity with MLOps tooling: experiment tracking, model versioning, feature stores, or inference serving frameworks.



About Nascent

Founded in 2020, Nascent exists to build, expand, and capture opportunity, in open markets and permissionless technologies. Building from a base of permanent capital, we deploy assets across a range of both liquid and long-term strategies that ensure we are among the most active users of the open financial system we are helping to build. We’ve backed 100+ early-stage teams https://www.nascent.xyz/portfolio that we believe can change markets and expand what’s possible. At Nascent, we combine that venture + market pedigree with product-grade engineering to turn research into revenue: experiments that ship fast, are evaluated rigorously, and move P&L. We focus on models, low-latency infrastructure, and resilient systems that compound performance over time.

Our Team & Culture

We’re an interdisciplinary team of engineers, quants, and operators who treat skill-building like a sport. The culture is competition + curiosity: push to win, share learnings, and iterate publicly through red-teams, internal games, and post-mortems. Autonomy isn’t a perk — it’s how we ship.

Principles that drive our team & work

 - Compete to win

 - Explore, experiment, play

 - Always be building

 - Seek and speak truth

 - Own your shit.



What We Offer

 - The opportunity to learn, experiment and build in an entrepreneurial environment

 - Remote-first, distributed team with frequent in-person sprints and retreats.

 - Competitive total comp with strong bonus upside tied to performance.

 - Hardware & home-office stipend, conference and learning budgets.

 - Comprehensive health benefits (medical/dental/vision), life insurance.

 - Open vacation policy as well as flexible work hours and location

 - 16 weeks fully-paid parental leave + supported return to work.

 - Retirement matching, open vacation policy, flexible hours.

### Job description

Job Title Assistant Research Scientist Agency Texas A&M Agrilife Research Department Biochemistry & Biophysics Proposed Minimum Salary Commensurate Job Location College Station, Texas Job Type Staff Job Description About Texas A&M AgriLife Texas A&M AgriLife is comprised of the following Texas A&M University System members: Texas A&M AgriLife Extension Service Texas A&M AgriLife Research College of Agriculture and Life Sciences at Texas A&M University Texas A&M Forest Service Texas A&M Veterinary Medical Diagnostic Laboratory As the nation’s largest most comprehensive agriculture program, Texas A&M AgriLife brings together a college and four state agencies focused on agriculture and life sciences within The Texas A&M University System. With over 5,000 employees and a presence in every county across the state, Texas A&M AgriLife is uniquely positioned to improve lives, environments and the Texas economy through education, research, extension and service. Click here to learn more about joining AgriLife! Position Information Job Summary: The Assistant Research Scientist performs independent or supervisory roles in research activities requiring an advanced level of knowledge in a field of biological science, are predominantly intellectual and varied in character, and require consistent use of discretion and judgment. Operation of research equipment, supervision of students and other lab personnel in research. Responsibilities: Design, plan, conduct and coordinate experiments in support of various research projects related to lipids and membrane trafficking in C. elegans. Use computers and laboratory or technical equipment to perform data analysis, statistical analysis, and technical evaluation of research experiments and results. Evaluate, select, and apply standard techniques of routine research; keep detailed records of data. Summarize results for inclusion in scientific and clientele-oriented publications. Supervise undergraduate and/or graduate students and other laboratory, or technical staff involved in research. Install, operate and/or repair research equipment. Order consumables and equipment, maintain financial accounts related to research projects. Perform other duties as assigned. Required Qualifications: PhD degree in biological sciences or related field. Some professional experience. Ability to effectively utilize a computer and applicable software to create databases, perform statistical analyses, present data and perform other computer related tasks, such as Microsoft Office, ImageJ and Matlab. Familiarity with genetic model systems, molecular biology, microscopy, biochemistry, and other appropriate laboratory and/or technical equipment. Good communication skills. Ability to multitask and work cooperatively with an International team of researchers. Ability to multitask and work cooperatively with others. Preferred Qualifications: Three years of laboratory experience in related field. Other Requirements: Work beyond normal business hours and/or work on weekends. What You Need to Know Salary: Compensation is commensurate with the candidate’s qualifications. Position Funding: This position is funded through grants or contracts. Continued employment is dependent upon ongoing funding and satisfactory job performance. Flexible Work Arrangements: This position requires on-site presence and is not eligible for remote work. Flexible work schedules may be available depending on the nature of the role. Important Notice Regarding H1-B Sponsorship: Your employment at Texas A&M AgriLife may require nonimmigrant sponsorship. A Presidential proclamation issued on September 19, 2025, imposes a $100,000 fee on new H-1B petitions filed after September 21, 2025. Texas A&M AgriLife will NOT pay this fee. If the fee is imposed on your petition, this offer of employment will be revoked. If actions taken during your employment result in the imposition of this fee, your employment will be terminated. To the extent that this statement conflicts with any applicable System or member Policy, Regulation, or Rule, this statement will control. We recommend that you consult with your private immigration counsel at your own expense regarding potential implications. In addition, on January 27, 2026, Texas Governor Abbott issued a moratorium on the filing of any new H-1B unless approved by the Texas Workforce Commission. Accordingly, if you will now or in the future require sponsorship for employment visa status this moratorium may affect our ability to employ you should you be selected as the final candidate. Benefits and Leave: Health, dental, vision, life and long-term disability insurance (AgriLife contributes to premiums) 12–15 annual paid holidays Up to 8 hours/month sick leave and at least 8 hours/month vacation Automatic enrollment in the Teacher Retirement System of Texas Employee Wellness Initiative Access to ongoing professional development and training through Aspire, LinkedIn Learning, and internally developed programs How to Apply Applications must include all job application data or a complete resume. Incomplete applications may be rejected. Applicants are encouraged to upload a resume or use LinkedIn to prepopulate application fields. Required Documents: CV / Resume List of References All positions are security-sensitive. Applicants are subject to a criminal history investigation, and employment is contingent upon the institution’s verification of credentials and/or other information required by the institution’s procedures, including the completion of the criminal history check. Equal Opportunity/Veterans/Disability Employer.

### Job description

Our Mission At Palo Alto Networks®, we’re united by a shared mission—to protect our digital way of life. We thrive at the intersection of innovation and impact, solving real-world problems with cutting-edge technology and bold thinking. Here, everyone has a voice, and every idea counts. If you’re ready to do the most meaningful work of your career alongside people who are just as passionate as you are, you’re in the right place. Who We Are In order to be the cybersecurity partner of choice, we must trailblaze the path and shape the future of our industry. This is something our employees work at each day and is defined by our values: Disruption, Collaboration, Execution, Integrity, and Inclusion. We weave AI into the fabric of everything we do and use it to augment the impact every individual can have. If you are passionate about solving real-world problems and ideating beside the best and the brightest, we invite you to join us! We believe collaboration thrives in person. That’s why most of our teams work from the office full time, with flexibility when it’s needed. This model supports real-time problem-solving, stronger relationships, and the kind of precision that drives great outcomes. Job Summary We are seeking a versatile Senior Data Scientist to join our team and help shape the future of our business operations. This role is a unique hybrid of classical machine learning and applied AI. You will tackle diverse and impactful projects, from building predictive models that drive key business decisions to characterizing and optimizing the performance of our emerging AI agentic systems. You are an entrepreneurial problem-solver who thrives in ambiguous environments and enjoys bridging the gap between technical execution and business strategy. In this role, you will act as both a builder, developing and deploying ML models, and a strategic partner, consulting with business teams to unlock new opportunities with data and AI. If you are passionate about wearing multiple hats, working in a fast-paced setting, and delivering high-quality, impactful work, we want to hear from you. This is an in-office role 3 days/week in our HQ, Santa Clara, CA . Key responsibilities Develop & Deploy Classical ML Models: Own the end-to-end lifecycle of machine learning projects. You'll build and productionize sophisticated models for critical business areas such as marketing attribution, customer churn prediction, and finance forecasting. Optimize AI Agentic Systems: Play a key role in our generative AI initiatives. You will be responsible for characterizing, evaluating, and fine-tuning AI agents—such as conversational systems that allow users to query massive datasets using natural language—to improve their accuracy, efficiency, and reliability. Partner with Business Stakeholders: Act as an internal consultant to our marketing, product, finance and go-to-market teams. You'll translate business challenges into data science problems, identify opportunities for AI-driven solutions, and present your findings in a clear, actionable manner. Own the Full Data Science Lifecycle: Your responsibilities will cover the entire project workflow, working with the business to understand the problem, chart a path to solve the problem, feature engineering, model selection and training, robust evaluation, deployment, and in partnership with the data platform team , ongoing monitoring for performance degradation. Data Storytelling: Don’t just build the model; sell the solution. You will visualize your findings (using Tableau, Looker, or custom coding) to make complex data intuitive for business stakeholders. Collaboration: Partner closely with Marketing and Product teams to translate their business goals into technical data requirements. Qualifications Required Qualifications Education & Experience: Bachelor’s degree with 3+ years of related experience; OR Master’s degree with 1+ years of related experience; OR PhD in a quantitative field (Computer Science, AI, Statistics, Physics, etc.) with 6+ months of experience (academic research applies). Strong foundation in statistics and probability theory and commonly used data science algorithms. Proven hands-on experience building and deploying machine learning models in a production environment. Expert-level programming skills in Python and familiarity with core data science and machine learning libraries (e.g., Scikit-learn, Pandas, PyTorch/TensorFlow, XGBoost). A solid command of SQL for complex querying and data manipulation. Proven ability to work autonomously, navigate ambiguity, and drive projects from concept to completion. Prior experience developing dashboards in Tableau/Looker or similar data viz platform. Preferred Qualifications Direct experience with generative AI, including hands-on work with LLMs and frameworks like LangChain, LlamaIndex, or the Hugging Face ecosystem. Experience in evaluating and optimizing the performance of AI systems or agents. Demonstrated expertise in specialized modeling domains such as causal inference, time-series analysis, or marketing mix modeling. Compensation Disclosure The compensation offered for this position will depend on qualifications, experience, and work location. For candidates who receive an offer at the posted level, the starting base salary (for non-sales roles) or base salary + commission target (for sales/com-missioned roles) is expected to be the annual range listed below. The offered compensation may also include restricted stock units and a bonus. A description of our employee benefits may be found here . $129,200.00 - $209,000.00/yr Our Commitment We’re trailblazers that dream big, take risks, and challenge cybersecurity’s status quo. It’s simple: we can’t accomplish our mission without diverse teams innovating, together. We are committed to providing

### Job description

Senior Associate, Data Scientist - Consumer Credit Risk Models and Data Data is at the center of everything we do. As a startup, we disrupted the credit card industry by individually personalizing every credit card offer using statistical modeling and the relational database, cutting edge technology in 1988! Fast-forward a few years, and this little innovation and our passion for data has skyrocketed us to a Fortune 200 company and a leader in the world of data-driven decision-making. As a Data Scientist at Capital One, you’ll be part of a team that’s leading the next wave of disruption at a whole new scale, using the latest in computing and machine learning technologies and operating across billions of customer records to unlock the big opportunities that help everyday people save money, time and agony in their financial lives. Team Description Have you ever seen the headline in the news “ Banks Pass Federal Reserve Stress Tests ” and wondered how Capital One determines how much savings (or “capital”) it needs? Or maybe how we analyze the potential impact of the next recession? At the heart of these questions are sophisticated econometric loss models that help us understand the ways in which the economy impacts our loan portfolios and guide strategic decision making at the highest levels of Capital One. In the Consumer Credit Risk Management Models and Data Team, we blend cutting-edge quantitative methods, with a deep understanding of our business, data, and regulatory environment to build and deploy predictive models for losses, account volumes and outstanding balances. These models drive key strategic decisions for loss allowances, stress testing, and capital allocation as well as informing our earnings calls and recession preparedness. If this sounds interesting to you, join us! As a Data Scientist on the deployment & platform side of the team, you’ll be at the forefront helping us to usher in the next wave of disruption by using the latest technology to deploy, optimize and modernize model pipelines and execution platforms that enable machine learning models to provide powerful insights about our portfolio and growth opportunities through new data sources. You will partner with best-in-class data scientists, analysts, and engineers to innovate solutions that directly impact the company’s bottom line in a meaningful way. You will do it all in a collaborative environment that values your insight, encourages you to take on new responsibilities, promotes continuous learning, and rewards innovation. Role Description In this role, you will: Partner with a cross-functional team of data scientists, software engineers, and product managers to deliver a product customers love Leverage a broad stack of technologies — Python, Conda, AWS, H2O, Spark, and more — to reveal the insights hidden within huge volumes of numeric and textual data Build machine learning models through all phases of development, from design through training, evaluation, validation, and implementation Flex your interpersonal skills to translate the complexity of your work into tangible business goals The Ideal Candidate is: Innovative. You continually research and evaluate emerging technologies. You stay current on published state-of-the-art methods, technologies, and applications and seek out opportunities to apply them. Creative. You thrive on bringing definition to big, undefined problems. You love asking questions and pushing hard to find answers. You’re not afraid to share a new idea. Technical. You’re comfortable with open-source languages and are passionate about developing further. You have hands-on experience developing data science solutions using open-source tools and cloud computing platforms. Statistically-minded. You’ve built models, validated them, and backtested them. You know how to interpret a confusion matrix or a ROC curve. You have experience with clustering, classification, sentiment analysis, time series, and deep learning. A data guru. “Big data” doesn’t faze you. You have the skills to retrieve, combine, and analyze data from a variety of sources and structures. You know understanding the data is often the key to great data science. Basic Qualifications: Currently has, or is in the process of obtaining one of the following with an expectation that the required degree will be obtained on or before the scheduled start date: A Bachelor's Degree in a quantitative field (Statistics, Economics, Operations Research, Analytics, Mathematics, Computer Science, or a related quantitative field) plus 2 years of experience performing data analytics A Master's Degree in a quantitative field (Statistics, Economics, Operations Research, Analytics, Mathematics, Computer Science, or a related quantitative field) or an MBA with a quantitative concentration Preferred Qualifications: Master’s Degree in “STEM” field (Science, Technology, Engineering, or Mathematics), or PhD in “STEM” field (Science, Technology, Engineering, or Mathematics) Experience working with AWS At least 2 years’ experience in Python, Scala, or R At least 2 years’ experience with machine learning At least 2 years’ experience with SQL Capital One will consider sponsoring a new qualified applicant for employment authorization for this position. The minimum and maximum full-time annual salaries for this role are listed below, by location. Please note that this salary information is solely for candidates hired to perform work within one of these locations, and refers to the amount Capital One is willing to pay at the time of this posting. Salaries for part-time roles will be prorated based upon the agreed upon number of hours to be regularly worked. McLean, VA: $135,600 - $154,800 for Sr Assoc, Data Science Candidates hired to work in other locations will be subject to the pay range associated with that location, and the actual annualized salary amount offered to any candidate at the time of hire will be reflected solely in the candidate’s offer letter. This role is also eligible to earn performance based incentive compensation, which may include cash bonus(es) and/or long term incentives (LTI). Incentives could be discretionary or non discretionary depending on the plan. Capital One offers a comprehensive, competitive, and inclusive set of health, financial and other benefits that support your total well-being. Learn more at the Capital One Careers website .
