- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

THUMBTACK HELPS MILLIONS OF PEOPLE CONFIDENTLY CARE FOR THEIR HOMES.

Thumbtack is the one app you need to take care of and improve your home — from personalized guidance to AI tools and a best-in-class hiring experience. Every day in every county of the U.S., people turn to Thumbtack to complete urgent repairs, seasonal maintenance and bigger improvements. We help homeowners know which projects to do, when to do them and who to hire from our growing community of 300,000 local service businesses. If making an impact inspires you, join us. Imagine what we’ll build together.


ABOUT THE DATA SCIENCE TEAM

The data science team empowers Thumbtack to use data to guide business decisions. We collaborate with partners across the company to solve the most analytically challenging problems -- everything from how we set prices across hundreds of local services categories, to evaluating the impact of a new business model on company performance, to identifying early predictors of high-quality customers. We are a tight-knit, intellectually curious team that is committed to improving Thumbtack and improving each other.




ABOUT THE ROLE

As a Marketing Data Scientist II at Thumbtack, you’ll be expected to leverage your analytical expertise, marketing knowledge, communication and collaboration skills to drive a variety of high-impact projects. You will embed directly with the paid marketing team to help optimize various marketing channels, drive personalization and discover new channels. Outside growth marketing work, you’ll contribute to our broader Data Science team and company data culture by sharing your learnings, partnering with others, and building new capabilities for how we can use data to guide decisions.




WHAT YOU’LL DO

 - Own end-to-end analytics for growth marketing, partnering with Marketing, Finance, and external partners to understand user behavior, define target audiences, measure campaign performance, and forecast and allocate budgets.

 - Translate ambiguous business questions into rigorous analyses and clear recommendations that improve individual campaign performance and optimize the overall marketing channel mix.

 - Design, execute, and interpret experiments and quasi-experiments to enable causal inference and inform high-stakes marketing investment decisions.

 - Partner closely with MarTech, Product Management, and Engineering to scope, build, and evaluate new capabilities—such as developing local market intelligence framework and improvements to customer LTV bidding systems—ensuring measurable performance gains.

 - Develop deep domain expertise in growth marketing measurement, experimentation, customer segmentation, and targeting optimization, and apply that expertise to influence strategy and execution.

 - Collaborate with cross-functional partners to build scalable analytical solutions, tools, and frameworks that unlock sustained growth.

 - Raise the bar for Thumbtack’s data culture by designing, maintaining, and documenting high-quality, trusted data tables and metrics that enable self-serve decision-making across teams.


IN ORDER TO BE SUCCESSFUL, YOU MUST BRING

 - Bachelor’s degree in statistics, mathematics, data science, economics, or a related quantitative field (advanced degree a plus).

 - 3+ years of experience in marketing data science, strong understanding of digital marketing and experience with marketing measurement and performance optimization.

 - Advanced SQL skills, proficiency in Python or R, and experience using Looker or similar BI tools to drive insights.

 - Experience working with large and imperfect datasets at scale, and building and applying statistical and/or machine learning models to solve real-world business problems.

 - A passion for problem-solving and critical thinking, with the ability to navigate ambiguity and turn data into clear, actionable insights.

 - Strong business judgment and the ability to identify signal in noisy, imperfect data.

 - Excellent communicator and compelling storyteller, with the ability to listen deeply, explain complex concepts clearly, and influence cross-functional partners.


EXPECTED SALARY RANGES

 - For candidates living in San Francisco / Bay Area, San Jose, New York City, or Seattle metros, the expected total cash compensation (base salary + variable pay, if applicable) range for the role is currently $197,200.00 - $255,200.00.

 - For candidates living in Austin, TX or Washington DC metros or in California, Massachusetts, New Jersey, or Washington states, the expected total cash compensation (base salary + variable pay, if applicable) range for the role is currently $177,700.00 - $229,900.00.

 - For candidates living in all other US locations, the expected total cash compensation (base salary + variable pay, if applicable) range for this role is currently $167,500.00 - $216,700.00.

Actual offered salaries will vary and will be based on various factors, such as calibrated job level, qualifications, skills, competencies, and proficiency for the role.

Thumbtack embraces diversity. We are proud to be an equal opportunity workplace and do not discriminate on the basis of sex, race, color, age, pregnancy, sexual orientation, gender identity or expression, religion, national origin, ancestry, citizenship, marital status, military or veteran status, genetic information, disability status, or any other characteristic protected by federal, provincial, state, or local law. Tools & Systems Preferred experience with BlackRock’s Aladdin, Bloomberg, and Macrobond for data extraction, portfolio monitoring, and analytics. Soft Skills Tenacious problem-solving abilities with a focus on efficiency and effectiveness. Strong attention to detail and passion for learning. Excellent communication skills and the ability to work collaboratively in a fast-paced environment. Demonstrated interest in investments and a desire to delve into the intricacies of fixed-income markets. With 5,400+ employees and over 80,000 institutional, commercial, and payments clients, we operate from more than 80 offices spread across six continents. As a Fortune 100, Nasdaq-listed provider, we connect clients to the global markets – focusing on innovation, human connection, and providing world-class products and services to all types of investors. Whether you want to forge a career connecting our retail clients to potential trading opportunities, or ingrain yourself in the world of institutional investing, StoneX Group is made up of four business segments that offer endless potential for progression and growth. Business Segment Overview: Corporate: Engage in a deep variety of business-critical activities that keep our company running efficiently. From strategic marketing and financial management to human resources and operational oversight, you’ll have the opportunity to optimize processes and implement game-changing policies. Position purpose: StoneX Group Inc. is seeking an experienced Senior Operational Risk Analyst to lead the program for all North America legal entities out of our Chicago office. Reporting to the Operational Risk Manager. The Senior Operational Risk Analyst is responsible for supporting the execution and ongoing enhancement of the organization's Operational Risk Management framework. The role will have primary responsibilities across Risk and Control Self-Assessments (RCSAs), Incident Management, control testing, independent review and challenge, and operational risk governance and reporting. The Senior Operational Risk Analyst will partner with business and functional stakeholders to identify and assess operational risks, evaluate the design and effectiveness of controls, analyze operational risk incidents, challenge risk assessments and remediation activities, and provide management with timely and meaningful risk information. The role requires strong analytical skills, sound risk judgment, attention to detail, and the ability to translate risk information into actionable insights for business leaders, Risk Management, and governance committees. Responsibilities Primary duties will include: Risk and Control Self-Assessments (RCSA) Coordinate and execute RCSA activities across assigned businesses and functions in accordance with the company's Operational Risk framework and methodology. Partner with business stakeholders to identify and assess key processes, operational risks, and controls. Facilitate RCSA workshops and risk discussions with business and functional stakeholders. Evaluate inherent risk, control effectiveness, residual risk, and alignment with established risk appetite and assessment criteria. Review the completeness and quality of risk and control documentation and supporting evidence. Provide independent review and challenge of business risk assessments, control evaluations, and residual risk conclusions. Identify inconsistencies, emerging risks, control weaknesses, and opportunities to strengthen the risk and control environment. Monitor RCSA remediation activities and ensure actions are appropriately documented and tracked through completion. Support ongoing enhancements to RCSA methodology, guidance, tools, and reporting. Operational Risk Incident Management Support the identification, assessment, documentation, escalation, and reporting of operational risk incidents and losses. Partner with business owners to ensure incidents are recorded accurately, completely, and in a timely manner. Review incident classifications, risk taxonomy mapping, financial and non-financial impacts, root causes, control failures, and remediation actions. Facilitate or challenge root cause analysis for significant or recurring incidents. Assess whether incidents indicate broader control weaknesses, emerging risks, or potential systemic issues. Identify trends and recurring themes across incidents and provide recommendations for risk mitigation or enhanced controls. Monitor incident remediation activities through completion and challenge whether actions sufficiently address identified root causes. Support escalation of material incidents in accordance with established governance and reporting requirements. Assess whether incidents should trigger reassessment of related RCSA risks, controls, or other elements of the Operational Risk framework. Control Testing & Assurance Plan, coordinate, and execute risk-based control testing in accordance with established control testing methodology and standards. Assess both the design and operating effectiveness of key controls. Define testing procedures, evidence requirements, sampling approaches, and evaluation criteria. Obtain and evaluate supporting evidence to determine whether controls are operating consistently and as designed. Maintain complete and defensible testing documentation, including testing procedures, evidence reviewed, exceptions identified, and conclusions reached. Clearly document identified control deficiencies and communicate results to relevant stakeholders. Work with control owners to develop appropriate remediation activities for identified deficiencies. Track remediation actions and perform or support validation activities prior to closure. Identify common control themes and systemic weaknesses across business areas and processes. Support enhancements to control testing methodology, procedures, documentation standards, and quality assurance. Review & Challenge Provide objective and constructive second-line review and challenge of operational risk activities performed by the business. Challenge the appropriateness of risk ratings, control effectiveness assessments, remediation plans, risk acceptances, and closure conclusions. Evaluate whether risks have been identified and assessed consistently with the organization's risk taxonomy, methodology, and risk appetite. Review business-provided evidence to determine whether conclusions are adequately supported. Escalate material concerns, overdue actions, recurring control weaknesses, or areas outside risk appetite through appropriate governance channels. Document review and challenge activities to provide a clear record of the rationale, evidence, conclusions, and management responses. Balance effective challenge with constructive partnership to support sustainable improvements within the business. Operational Risk Governance & Reporting Prepare regular Operational Risk rep [...]

We advance science so that we all have more time with the people we love. Your work will help bridge scientific intuition with quantitative analysis, enable richer phenotypic analysis, and provide new multimodal capabilities across large-scale, state-of-the-art datasets. You'll build, own, and constantly improve scalable AI/ML and computer vision systems that unlock the potential of our diverse scientific imaging data (e.g., microscopy, digital pathology, biomedical imaging, etc), accelerating the discovery and development of life-changing treatments for patients. The Opportunity: · Design, develop, and test robust, scalable, and maintainable AI/ML scientific web applications and backend pipelines tailored for high-throughput scientific imaging data processing and visualization. · Build, evaluate, and fine-tune state-of-the-art computer vision and deep learning models (e.g., cell segmentation, cell classification, phenotypic profiling, tissue segmentation, feature extraction, and image registration) applied to biological and clinical imaging modalities. · Build tools to evaluate AI/ML and vision model performance, establish benchmark datasets, and quantify model quality, robustness, and drift over time. · Partner with product managers, bioimage analysts, and research scientists to understand user workflows, shape requirements, and translate complex scientific imaging needs into actionable technical specifications. · Develop and maintain scalable cloud infrastructures and data pipelines for collecting, structuring, storing, and accessing large-scale multi-dimensional imaging datasets (e.g., OME-TIFF, Zarr, HDF5). · Optimize GPU memory footprint, batching, and inference throughput for large image pipelines and deep neural network models. · Contribute to architectural decisions, code reviews, and the evolution of engineering and MLOps processes for computer vision models. · Be willing to span the full stack and contribute where needed, even outside of core areas of expertise. · Stay up-to-date with emerging computer vision technologies and industry best practices while promoting a culture of continuous learning, collaboration, and curiosity. Who You Are · PhD, Master’s with 3–5 years, or Bachelor’s with 4–7 years experience with a degree in Computer Science, Biomedical Engineering, Electrical Engineering, Computational Biology, or a related technical field with focus on machine learning engineering and computer vision. · Strong proficiency with AI/ML frameworks and computer vision libraries (e.g., PyTorch, TensorFlow, OpenCV, MONAI, Scikit-Image, torchvision, Napari, or Bio-Formats). · Deep expertise in computer vision algorithms (e.g., CNNs, Vision Transformers, UNet, SAM/Segment Anything, Cellpose). · Expert knowledge of machine learning theory, image processing fundamentals, object detection, instance segmentation, and quantitative image analysis. · Demonstrated experience working with high-dimensional scientific imaging data (e.g., fluorescence/brightfield microscopy, digital pathology whole slide images, high-content screening, multiplexed imaging, etc). · Strong knowledge of GPU acceleration, parallel computing, CUDA, and ML performance optimization for large image datasets. · Experience with Kubernetes, relational databases, NoSQL databases, object storage, and cloud-native architectures in public clouds (ideally AWS). · Proven understanding and application of software engineering best practices, CI/CD, and MLOps principles. · Excellent communication skills and ability to build trusted partnerships with scientific collaborators, bioimage analysts, and cross-functional teams. · Ability to quickly acquire new technologies, domain knowledge, and programming languages with a passion for continuous learning. Preferred Qualifications · Experience with multi-modal data fusion (combining scientific imaging with genomics, transcriptomics, or clinical tabular data). · Experience working directly with biomedical scientists or in a pharma/biotech research environment. · Experience with workflow automation, GenAI, Vision-Language Models, and/or AI agents. Onsite presence on our South San Francisco campus is expected for at least 3 days a week. Relocation Assistance benefits are not available for this posting. The expected salary range for this position based on the primary location of San Franscico, CA is $168,100- $312,300 Annual. This position is open to both SE6 and SE7 levels, with final leveling to be determined upon completion of the interview process. Actual pay will be determined based on experience, qualifications, geographic location, and other job-related factors permitted by law. A discretionary annual bonus may be available based on individual and Company performance. This position also qualifies for the benefits detailed here: Benefits Genentech

### Job description

Interest-based E-commerce is a new and fast growing business that aims at connecting all customers' interests to excellent sellers and high quality products on TikTok Shop. Different from other traditional E-commerce platforms, TikTok Shop provides customers with personalized and unique shopping experience through E-commerce live-streaming and E-commerce short videos. The recommendation system plays an extremely important role in helping customers explore their shopping interests. 

We are a group of applied machine learning engineers and research scientists that focus on E-commerce video/live-streaming recommendations on the major traffic source of TikTok ForU page, where we serve traffic for billions of users every single day. We develop innovative algorithms and ML techniques to improve user engagement and satisfaction, converting creative ideas into business-impacting solutions. We are interested and excited about applying large scale machine learning to solve various real-world problems in E-commerce and recommendation.

Responsibilities
- Participate in building large-scale (10 million to 100 million) live-streaming and short video e-commerce recommendation algorithms and systems on TikTok.
- Design, develop, evaluate and iterate on predictive models for candidate generation and ranking(eg. Click Through Rate and Conversion Rate prediction) , including, but not limited to building real-time data pipelines, feature engineering, model optimization and innovation.
- Build long and short term user interest models, analyze and extract relevant information from large amounts of various data and design algorithms to explore users' latent interests efficiently.
- Design and develop various strategies using ML technology to improve user shopping experience, and resolve e-commerce business challenges, such as the cold start problem and traffic allocation.
- Design and build supporting/debugging tools as needed. Minimum Qualifications
- Bachelor's degree or higher in Computer Science or related fields. 
- Strong programming and problem-solving ability. 
- 3 years of experience in applied machine learning, familiar with one or more of the algorithms such as Collaborative Filtering, Matrix Factorization, Factorization Machines, Word2vec, Logistic Regression, Gradient Boosting Trees, Deep Neural Networks, Wide and Deep etc. 
- Experience in  Deep Learning Tools such as tensorflow/pytorch.
- Experience with at least one programming language like C++/Python or equivalent.

Preferred Qualifications
- 3 years of experience in recommendation system, online advertising, information retrieval, natural language processing, machine learning, large-scale data mining, or related fields.
- Publications at KDD, NeurlPS, WWW, SIGIR, WSDM, ICML, IJCAI, AAAI, RECSYS and  related conferences/journals, or experience in data mining/machine learning competitions such as Kaggle/KDD-cup etc.

- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

Position Summary The Senior Data Engineer - Databricks & Data Modeling will develop, deploy, and maintain production data pipelines and reusable data models within Winsupply's Databricks lakehouse on Google Cloud. This hands-on role combines strong Databricks engineering skills with practical relational and dimensional modeling expertise to convert complex source data into trusted Silver- and Gold-layer data products. Working within the Data Architecture & Engineering team, this individual will implement approved architectural standards, collaborate with business stakeholders, Data Governance, and IT, and take ownership of reliable, efficient delivery from source ingestion through production support. Accountabilities/Responsibilities Build, test, deploy, and support production ingestion and transformation pipelines using Databricks, Delta Lake, Unity Catalog, Apache Spark, Python/PySpark, and Databricks SQL. Independently design and implement relational and dimensional data models for assigned data products, including fact tables, conformed dimensions, star schemas, and slowly changing dimensions (SCD Type 1/2). Translate core business processes and relationships among Customer, Product, Vendor, Purchasing, Inventory, Sales, and Pricing entities into accurate Silver-layer structures and reusable Gold-layer data products, working with Data Architecture leadership on complex model decisions. Ingest and integrate batch and streaming data from ERP, relational, legacy/mainframe, fixed-width or delimited files, APIs, and cloud storage; handle schema changes, incremental loads, and CDC as appropriate. Implement business keys, table grain, joins, deduplication, history handling, and source-to-target mappings that produce repeatable, traceable results. Create and automate data quality checks, reconciliation, pipeline monitoring, alerting, exception handling, and recovery procedures for production operations. Tune Spark workloads, Databricks SQL queries, Delta table layouts, job configuration, and compute usage to improve performance, reliability, and cost efficiency. Develop reusable, maintainable code with Git, peer reviews, unit/integration tests, CI/CD, and repeatable Databricks deployment practices. Partner with business process owners and Data Governance to understand operational workflows, validate entity definitions and business rules, and use that context to inform sound data modeling, quality, lineage, and access decisions. Document pipelines, models, transformation logic, dependencies, and runbooks so solutions can be maintained and supported by the broader team. Troubleshoot data defects and pipeline failures, identify root causes, and drive fixes through production with appropriate communication and ownership. Collaborate with the architecture team, DaaS, IT, and delivery partners; contribute practical recommendations and mentor peers while supporting approved technical direction. Competencies for Success Has delivered and supported Databricks pipelines and data products in production from raw ingestion through trusted analytical outputs. Demonstrates strong modeling judgment grounded in business process knowledge: understands core entities, grain, keys, relationships, SCD Type 1/2, fact/dimension design, and reporting impacts. Troubleshoots distributed data processing, data-quality discrepancies, and performance issues with a structured, evidence-based approach. Communicates clearly, challenges proposed solutions constructively, and collaborates across technical and business teams. Takes ownership of code quality, documentation, reliability, cost awareness, and delivery commitments. Qualifications/Experience Minimum Qualifications 7+ years of related professional experience in data engineering, data warehousing, analytics engineering, or comparable roles. 3+ years of hands-on Databricks production experience with Delta Lake, Unity Catalog, Spark/PySpark, and Databricks SQL. 3+ years of meaningful relational or dimensional data modeling experience, including personally designing and deploying fact/dimension or integrated business models. Foundational understanding of core business processes and entities (e.g., Customer, Product, Vendor, Purchasing, Inventory, Sales, and Pricing), with the ability to validate relationships with business stakeholders and apply that context to data modeling and governance decisions. Experience building production ETL/ELT pipelines, implementing incremental processing, and supporting operational data quality and monitoring. Strong SQL and Python/PySpark skills, with hands-on Git, testing, code review, and CI/CD practices. Associate degree in Computer Science, Information Systems, or a related field; equivalent professional experience or relevant technical certifications may be considered. Preferred Qualifications Bachelor's degree in Computer Science, Information Systems, Data Engineering, or a related discipline. Databricks Certified Data Engineer Professional or comparable Databricks certification. Experience with Databricks on Google Cloud, Lakeflow pipelines/jobs, Auto Loader, CDC, and Declarative Automation Bundles. Kimball dimensional modeling, enterprise data governance, semantic modeling for Looker, or advanced Spark performance optimization. Experience integrating IBM/mainframe, DB2, distribution/supply-chain, financial, or pricing data. - Title scope: core | Has: llm, python, regression, ai agent, git, kubernetes, pipeline, publication, research | Lacks: -  | Note: title says Senior/II: asks 3 years
- Eligibility: NONE (no sponsorship or authorization sentence found: still read the employer page)

### Job description

About Us: Proofpoint is a global leader in human- and agent-centric cybersecurity. We protect how people, data, and AI agents connect across email, cloud, and collaboration tools. Over 80 of the Fortune 100, 10,000 large enterprises, and millions of smaller organizations trust Proofpoint to stop threats, prevent data loss, and build resilience across their people and AI workflows. Our mission is simple: safeguard the digital world and empower people to work securely and confidently. Join us in our pursuit to defend data and protect people. How We Work: At Proofpoint you’ll be part of a global team that breaks barriers to redefine cybersecurity guided by our BRAVE core values: Bold in how we dream and innovate Responsive to feedback, challenges and opportunities Accountable for results and best in class outcomes Visionary in future focused problem-solving Exceptional in execution and impact The Role AI is moving faster than any single product team can keep up with. For a security company, the hard question isn’t “can a model do this?” It’s which approach actually works, what it costs, how fast and reliable it is, and whether it’s worth building at all. That’s what this team answers. We’re an Applied AI Research team , and research is our product. We take important questions the company can’t yet answer and turn them into evidence people can act on. As a Senior Applied AI Engineer , you’ll own a line of research from start to finish: frame the question, build what’s needed to answer it, measure honestly, and reach a decision. When something is ready to move to another team, you’ll help them deploy it well, advising on the AI side and on how to monitor it once it’s live. Sometimes the answer is “this works, here’s the proof.” Sometimes it’s “this won’t work, and we found out in three weeks instead of six months.” Both count as success here. What you'll do Answer hard questions with evidence. Take a question nobody has answered yet. Make it testable, set a baseline, run the smallest experiment that settles it, and come back with an answer the company can act on. Build systems that improve themselves. A lot of our agentic work runs in long loops: run an evaluation, study the results, produce a better version, run it again. You’ll build systems that improve on their own and recover when they go wrong. Make agent behavior measurable. Extend our evaluation platform so we can tell whether an agent is genuinely good - task suites, regression checks, and honest tradeoffs between quality, cost and speed. It’s something we build and maintain for the whole company. Work across hosted and self-hosted models. Most of what we do runs on third-party model APIs. We also serve open-weight models on our own GPUs and are pushing further into fine-tuning and efficient serving. You’ll help us work out which jobs suit which. Improve how we build with coding agents. We use coding agents every day and benchmark them against our own codebases. You’ll help our engineers get more out of them. Start from real problems. Spend time with stakeholders, understand their problems rather than collecting feature requests, and turn what you hear into research worth doing. Put working things in people’s hands. Build prototypes, playgrounds and APIs so stakeholders can try an idea themselves and learn alongside us. Often that’s how the decision gets made. Help get it into production. When something works, we don’t just hand it over. You’ll advise the team deploying it - on the AI side, and on how to monitor and understand the system once it’s running. Help the team get better. Mentor engineers, share what you learn, and help the team ask better questions. What we're looking for Bachelors, Masters or PhD in Computer Science or related field or equivalent experience 5+ years building software and ML systems, with recent hands-on work on LLM systems that went past the prototype stage, 7+ years preferred. 3+ years of research experience (understanding customer problems and deriving/brainstorming solutions) Experience with Claude Code, Cursor or similar, used daily, without lowering your standard for correctness and tests. Experience in building and deeply evaluating agentic systems in production Experience with third-party model APIs and open-weight models (we run on our own GPU fleet) Strong Python and solid ML fundamentals Demonstrated expertise in AI evaluation, one of the most challenging and strategically important areas of modern artificial intelligence Familiarity with Kubernetes inference platform, Flyte for orchestration, LiteLLM / Langfuse and internal tooling for evaluation and tracing Nice to Have Experience building open weights LLMs (not only classical (non-LLM) ML models) and evaluating the system to quantify business value Experience fine-tuning or serving open-weight models Experience with automated prompt and pipeline optimization Experience with multi-GPU serving and or MCP and tool protocols Previous cybersecurity experience such as phishing or threat detection Open-source contributions or publications Why Proofpoint? At Proofpoint, we believe that an exceptional career experience includes a comprehensive compensation and benefits package. Here are just a few reasons you’ll love working with us: Competitive compensation Comprehensive benefits Career success on your terms Flexible work environment Annual wellness and community outreach days Always on recognition for your contributions Global collaboration and networking opportunities Our Culture: Our culture is rooted in values that inspire belonging, empower purpose and drive success-every day, for everyone. We encourage applications from individuals of all backgrounds, experiences, and perspectives. If you need accommodation during the application or interview process, please reach out to accessibility@proofpoint.com . How to Apply Interested? Submit your application along with any supporting information- we can’t wait to hear from you!
